    def test_at_i12_2_rail_keys_and_issue_peek_after_the_aliases_go(tmp_path: Path, case08: Path) -> None:
        """AT-I12-2 (US-I12-1; rows R-SHELL-KEYS.3, R-TUI-021.3, R-ISSUES-HEXPANE-001.1):
        keys ``2``, ``3``, ``1`` show the A2L Explorer, the MAC View and the Workspace;
        Enter on the first Issues row whose address lies in the image keeps the Issues
        window shown and paints that address's hex row in the peek."""

        async def scenario(app, pilot) -> None:
            image = case08 / "firmware.s19"
            app.load_selected_file(image)
            await wait_until(
                pilot,
                lambda: app.current_file is not None and app.current_file.path.name == image.name,
                "the image load did not finish", timeout=LOAD_WAIT_S,
            )
            await settle_workers(pilot)
            app.load_a2l_from_path(case08 / "firmware_issues.a2l")
            await wait_until(
                pilot, lambda: app.current_a2l_data is not None, "the A2L load did not finish", timeout=LOAD_WAIT_S
            )
            await settle_workers(pilot)

                pilot, lambda: len(app._validation_issues) > 0, "no validation issues after the loads",
                timeout=LOAD_WAIT_S,
            )
            await settle(pilot)
            for key, window in (("2", "a2l"), ("3", "mac"), ("1", "workspace")):
                await pilot.press(key)
                await settled(
                    pilot, lambda: app._active_screen_key == window,
                    f"key {key} shows {window}; shown {app._active_screen_key}", timeout=CLOSE_WAIT_S,
                )
            await pilot.press("5")
            await settled(
                pilot, lambda: app._active_screen_key == "issues" and bool(list(app.query(IssueRow))),
                "the Issues window lists rows", timeout=CLOSE_WAIT_S,
            )
            mem_map = app.current_file.mem_map
            rows = [r for r in app.query(IssueRow) if isinstance(r.issue.address, int) and r.issue.address in mem_map]
            assert rows, "no listed issue has an address inside the image"
            row = rows[0]
            address = row.issue.address
            pane = app.query_one("#issues_hex_pane")
            before = str(pane.render())
            wanted = f"{address - address % 16:08X}"
            assert wanted not in before, before
            row.focus()
            await settle(pilot)
            await pilot.press("enter")
            await settled(
                pilot, lambda: wanted in str(pane.render()),
                f"the peek paints the row {wanted}; it shows {str(pane.render())!r}", timeout=CLOSE_WAIT_S,
            )
            assert app._active_screen_key == "issues"

                )

    async def wait_until(
        pilot,
        predicate: Callable[[], bool],
        failure: Union[str, Callable[[], str]],
        *,
        timeout: float = WAIT_TIMEOUT_S,
        pause_first: bool = False,
    ) -> None:
        """Poll ``predicate`` until it is truthy, yielding to the app between
        checks; raise ``AssertionError`` with ``failure`` at the deadline.

        A callable ``failure`` is called only when this raises, so its text can
        show state accumulated up to the timeout.
        """
        deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout
        if pause_first:
            await pilot.pause()
        while True:
            if predicate():
                return
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
>               raise AssertionError(failure() if callable(failure) else failure)
E               AssertionError: timed out waiting for #patch_entry_address_input after key '6'

    def test_canon_rows_hold_the_obligation_word_once() -> None:
        """R-DOC-CANON.4 -- each sentence holds ``shall`` exactly once.

        Why: one ``shall`` is one obligation is one test case. Two make a row that can be
        half met; none makes a statement that obliges nothing.
        """
        rows = _rows()
        offenders: List[str] = []
        for r in rows:
            n = len(re.findall(r"\bshall\b", r.sentence))
            if n != 1:
                offenders.append(f"line {r.line} {r.id}: 'shall' appears {n} times")
>       _report("R-DOC-CANON.4 the obligation word is not used exactly once", offenders, len(rows))

    def _report(rule: str, offenders: List[str], checked: int) -> None:
        if not offenders:
            return
        shown = "\n  ".join(offenders[:SHOWN])
        more = f"\n  ... and {len(offenders) - SHOWN} more" if len(offenders) > SHOWN else ""

        The write reaches the image only through the save-back: the document is
        applied with ``0x8013`` = ``11`` (the *Save patched image as* row shows only
        after an apply), the entry is edited to ``AA`` with no new apply, and
        *Write file* is confirmed (finding W-1: the case's "loaded, not applied"

            )

    async def wait_until(
        pilot,
        predicate: Callable[[], bool],
        failure: Union[str, Callable[[], str]],
        *,
        timeout: float = WAIT_TIMEOUT_S,
        pause_first: bool = False,
    ) -> None:
        """Poll ``predicate`` until it is truthy, yielding to the app between
        checks; raise ``AssertionError`` with ``failure`` at the deadline.

        A callable ``failure`` is called only when this raises, so its text can
        show state accumulated up to the timeout.
        """
        deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout
        if pause_first:
            await pilot.pause()
        while True:
            if predicate():
                return
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:

    def test_r_crc_preview_002_5_5_save_project_then_display(

    ) -> None:
        """Verifies R-CRC-PREVIEW-002.5/5: in an open project, after the apply of
        ``AA``, a display (``0x08DAAFB7``) and the entry edited to ``BB``, a Save
        project commit made while the CRC Designer is hidden (key ``1``) leaves 0
        recomputes until key ``0``; then exactly 1, with ``0x614BD0E8`` /
        ``0xE1E79AB1``.

        Why: the commit re-derives the image and writes BB in place; the painted AA
        value is stale. Base: the designer keeps ``0x08DAAFB7`` -> RED."""
        _set_quiet(monkeypatch, QUIET_SHORT_S)

        def make(folder: Path, image: Path):
            async def scenario(app, pilot, log):
                await _load_image_by_dialog(pilot, app, image)
                await _open_project(pilot, app, log)
                await _load_document(pilot, app, log, folder, "d.changes.json", [AA_ENTRY])
                assert await _apply(pilot, app, log, "Apply:") == "Apply: 1 applied, 0 skipped, 0 blocked"
                await _fresh_designer_display(pilot, app, AFTER_AA)
                await _entry_op(
                    pilot, app, log, "#patch_entry_edit_button", "0x8003", "BB", "Patch Editor: entry updated."
                )
                await _show_rail(pilot, app, "1", "#loaded_slots")
                before = designer_recomputes()
                await _save_project(pilot, app, log)
                assert app.current_file.mem_map[0x8003] == 0xBB, "the commit did not write the BB"
                await _quiet(pilot)
                assert designer_recomputes() == before, "a hidden CRC Designer recomputed"
                await _press_rail(pilot, app, "0")
                await _expect_refresh(pilot, app, before, AFTER_BB, "Save project then display")

            return scenario

    def test_tc081_2_strip(tmp_path: Path) -> None:

        ``Widget`` internals and produce a silent mount crash / idle boot deadlock

                    child.id

                return len(strips), order

tmp_path = WindowsPath('[USER_HOME]/Github/s19_app-b103-g3-repro-residual/head/main/tmp/test_at_b99_02_save_back_exclu0')

    def test_at_b99_02_save_back_excludes_s0(tmp_path):
        """Load case_01 through the dialog, rewrite in-image bytes, save back:
        the independent reader sees no byte at 0x0..0xB and identical data;
        the Memory Map lists no row carrying 0x00000000; a document whose only
        entry is at 0x0 shows that entry as skipped in the status line.

        The whole drive is keys and clicks (review finding F2): inputs are
        focused with ``pilot.click`` and typed with ``pilot.press``, buttons are
        clicked, and every wait observes the painted status line or the painted
        screen (``export_screenshot``) � never an internal attribute.
        """
        from textual.widgets import Button, Input

        src = tmp_path / "firmware.s19"
        shutil.copy(CASE01, src)
        oracle = read_s19(src)

        app = S19TuiApp(base_dir=tmp_path)

        async def _drive():
            # 54 rows: the Patch Editor's docked save-back row sits at the foot
            # of the JSON pane and is only clickable/painted at the taller
            # viewport (the b97 ATs drive the panel at 118x54 for the same row).
            async with app.run_test(size=(120, 54)) as pilot:
                await pilot.pause()

                async def _type(input_id: str, text: str) -> None:
                    assert await pilot.click(f"#{input_id}"), (
                        f"click missed #{input_id}"
                    )
                    await pilot.press(*text)
                # 1. Load through the Load file dialog (rail key ``l``).
                await pilot.press("l")
                for _ in range(6):
                    await pilot.pause()
                # The dialog is a pushed screen: click/type inside ``app.screen``.
                await _type("load_path", str(src))
                assert await pilot.click("#load_ok"), "click missed #load_ok"
                await _wait_for(
                    pilot,
                    lambda: "Loaded firmware.s19" in _status_text(app),
                    "load status line",
                )

                # 2. Patch Editor (rail key ``6``): one entry rewriting the two
                #    bytes already at the first data address (identity rewrite).
                await pilot.press("6")
                for _ in range(6):
                    await pilot.pause()
                addr = min(oracle.data)
                rewritten = [oracle.data[addr], oracle.data[addr + 1]]
                await _type("patch_entry_address_input", f"0x{addr:X}")
                await _type(
                    "patch_entry_bytes_input",
                    " ".join(f"0x{b:02X}" for b in rewritten),
                )
                assert await pilot.click(
                    "#patch_entry_add_button"
                ), "click missed #patch_entry_add_button"
                await _wait_for(
                    pilot,
                    lambda: "entry added" in _status_text(app),
                    "add status line",
                )
                assert await pilot.click(
                    "#patch_doc_apply_button"
                ), "click missed #patch_doc_apply_button"
                await _wait_for(
                    pilot,
                    lambda: "Apply:" in _status_text(app),
                    "apply status line",
                )
                assert "1 applied, 0 skipped, 0 blocked" in _status_text(app)

                # 3. Save-back (offered after the applied entry, premise P-23).
                #    The prompt renders in the editor's JSON pane: pick that tab
                #    by clicking its chip, then wait for the prompt's label to be
                #    PAINTED on the screen (the row reveal), instead of reading
                #    a CSS class.
                assert await pilot.click("#patch_tab_json"), (
                    "click missed #patch_tab_json"
                )
                await _wait_for(
                    pilot,
                    lambda: "patched" in _painted_text(app),
                    "save-back prompt painted",
                )
                # The prompt pre-fills the name: select all, replace by typing.
                assert await pilot.click(
                    "#patch_saveback_name_input"
                ), "click missed #patch_saveback_name_input"
                await pilot.press("ctrl+shift+a")
                await pilot.press(*"at_b99_02.s19")
                assert await pilot.click(
                    "#patch_saveback_confirm_button"
                ), "click missed #patch_saveback_confirm_button"
                target = None
                for _ in range(100):
                    candidates = sorted(tmp_path.rglob("at_b99_02.s19"))
                    if candidates:
                        target = candidates[0]
                        break
                    await pilot.pause(0.05)
                assert target is not None, "save-back wrote no file"

                written = read_s19(target)
                assert not any(a <= 0xB for a in written.data), (
                    f"bytes at 0x0..0xB: {sorted(a for a in written.data if a <= 0xB)}"
                )
                assert written.data == oracle.data
                assert written.header == oracle.header

                # 4. Memory Map (rail key ``4``): no row carries 0x00000000.
                #    Every row starts with a band glyph (``\u2591 0x00000000 \u2026``), so
                #    a startswith probe can never fire (review finding F1) � the
                #    address must be matched as a word anywhere in the row.  The
                #    rows fill without an explicit ``update_memory_map()`` call.
                await pilot.press("4")
                rows = []
                for _ in range(100):
                    await pilot.pause()
                    rows = [
                        str(r.render()).strip()
                        for r in app.query(".map-region-row")
                    ]
                    if rows:
                        break
                assert rows, "Memory Map lists no regions"
                assert not any(

                # 5. A document whose only entry is at 0x0: skipped in the

                            "kind": "change",

                await _type("patch_doc_path_input", str(doc_path))
                assert await pilot.click(
                    "#patch_doc_load_button"
                ), "click missed #patch_doc_load_button"
                # The added entry left the draft dirty (LLR-005.1 w1): Load asks
                # first � the operator accepts the loss on the guard modal.

                        break
                assert type(app.screen).__name__ == "LoadGuardScreen"
                # The modal is pushed before it is composed; wait for its accept
                # button to be painted, then click it.

                )
                assert await pilot.click(
                    "#load_guard_accept"
                ), "click missed #load_guard_accept"
                await _wait_for(
                    pilot,

                    lambda: _status_text(app) != previous,

                )
                status = _status_text(app)
                assert "0 applied, 1 skipped, 0 blocked" in status, status

    def test_r_crc_preview_002_5_7_blocked_save_back_still_refreshes(
        tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
    ) -> None:
        """Verifies R-CRC-PREVIEW-002.5/7 (E3 SEC5-2): after the apply of ``AA`` and
        one display (``0x08DAAFB7``), a second entry ``0x8002`` = ``11 22`` (it
        overlaps ``0x8003``) and *Save patched image as* ``fixture-blocked.s19``
        confirmed, the save ends ``Patched image not saved - 2 entries blocked - see
        issues``, writes no file, and leaves ``0x8002`` / ``0x8003`` at ``02`` /
        ``03``; the first display afterwards shows ``0x9C5BCBBD`` / ``0x2A8A3950``.

        Why: the refused save-back has already adopted the re-derived bytes (the
        revert), so a hook gated on the save's ``ok`` leaves the designer painting
        the AA value of an image that no longer holds it. Base: the designer keeps
        ``0x08DAAFB7`` -> RED. Negative control: hook under ``if result.ok`` -> RED."""
        _set_quiet(monkeypatch, QUIET_SHORT_S)

        def make(folder: Path, image: Path):
            async def scenario(app, pilot, log):
                await _load_image_by_dialog(pilot, app, image)
                await _load_document(pilot, app, log, folder, "d.changes.json", [AA_ENTRY])
                assert await _apply(pilot, app, log, "Apply:") == "Apply: 1 applied, 0 skipped, 0 blocked"
                await _fresh_designer_display(pilot, app, AFTER_AA)
                await _entry_op(
                    pilot, app, log, "#patch_entry_add_button", "0x8002", "11 22", "Patch Editor: entry added."
                )
                before = designer_recomputes()  # E5 C-3: before the trigger
                lines = await _save_back(
                    pilot,
                    app,
                    log,
                    "fixture-blocked.s19",
                    "Patched image not saved - 2 entries blocked - see issues",
                )
                assert any(m == "Patched image not saved - 2 entries blocked - see issues" for m in lines), lines
                await _quiet(pilot, 3)
                assert not list((folder / "app").rglob("fixture-blocked.s19")), "a blocked save-back wrote a file"
                mem = app.current_file.mem_map
                assert (mem[0x8002], mem[0x8003]) == (0x02, 0x03), (mem[0x8002], mem[0x8003])
                await _quiet(pilot)
                assert designer_recomputes() == before, "a hidden CRC Designer recomputed"
                await _press_rail(pilot, app, "0")
                await _expect_refresh(pilot, app, before, PRE, "blocked save-back then display")

            return scenario

    async def wait_until(
        pilot,
        predicate: Callable[[], bool],
        failure: Union[str, Callable[[], str]],
        *,
        timeout: float = WAIT_TIMEOUT_S,
        pause_first: bool = False,
    ) -> None:
        """Poll ``predicate`` until it is truthy, yielding to the app between
        checks; raise ``AssertionError`` with ``failure`` at the deadline.

        A callable ``failure`` is called only when this raises, so its text can
        show state accumulated up to the timeout.
        """
        deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout
        if pause_first:
            await pilot.pause()
        while True:
            if predicate():
                return
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
>               raise AssertionError(failure() if callable(failure) else failure)

________________ test_at076a_docked_buttons_are_grouped_chips _________________

    def test_at076a_docked_buttons_are_grouped_chips() -> None:
        """AT-076a � every docked Button is a chip; >=3 groups resolve distinctly.

        Intent (HLR-076): the analyst reads the docked buttons as colour-GROUPED
        chips, so function is legible before the label is. Asserted as a structural
        invariant enumerated from the live tree � "every docked Button carries a
        chip class AND >=3 distinct groups are present" � never "the N buttons all
        render as chips", which would be an unverified all-at-once claim (C-29).
        """

        async def _run(size: tuple[int, int]) -> dict[str, object]:
            app = S19TuiApp(base_dir=Path(_tmp(size)))
            async with app.run_test(size=size) as pilot:
                await pilot.pause()
                app.action_show_screen("patch")
                await pilot.pause()
                panel = app.query_one(f"#{_PANEL_ID}")
                buttons = list(panel.walk_children(Button))
                naked = [
                    b.id
                    for b in buttons
                    if _CHIP_CLASS not in b.classes and _TAB_CHIP_CLASS not in b.classes
                ]
                # One button from each of the three groups, through the REAL tree.
                # MEASURED (batch-94): the entry probe rides
                # ``#patch_entry_remove_button`` � ``#patch_entry_add_button`` /
                # ``#patch_entry_edit_button`` (the first grid row of
                # ``#patch_doc_entry_buttons``) paint at ZERO height under the
                # content-height chain, so their ``region.height`` cannot carry
                # the 1-row chip-shape assertion; Remove / Edit JSON paint.
                probes = {
                    "entry": app.query_one("#patch_entry_remove_button", Button),
                    "apply": app.query_one("#patch_doc_apply_button", Button),
                    "checks": app.query_one("#patch_checks_run_button", Button),
                }
                heights = {k: b.region.height for k, b in probes.items() if k != "checks"}
                # batch-97 Inc-4: Run checks lives on the inspector's Checks tab.
                app.query_one("#patch_tab_checks", Button).press()
                await pilot.pause()
                heights["checks"] = probes["checks"].region.height
                return {
                    "count": len(buttons),
                    "naked": naked,
                    "colours": {
                        k: b.styles.color.rgb for k, b in probes.items()
                    },
                    "heights": heights,
                }

        for size in _SIZES:
            r = asyncio.run(_run(size))
            assert r["count"] > 0, f"@{size}: no docked Buttons found at all"
            assert r["naked"] == [], (
                f"@{size}: {len(r['naked'])} docked Button(s) carry NO chip class "
                f"� the family must cover every one of them: {r['naked']}"
            )
            colours = r["colours"]
            assert len(set(colours.values())) == 3, (
                f"@{size}: the three chip groups must resolve to three DISTINCT "
                f"colours; got {colours}"
            )
            # The chip's own shape: one row, not Textual's 3-row default Button.
            for group, height in r["heights"].items():
>               assert height == 1, (
                    f"@{size}: the {group} chip must render as a single row "
                    f"(Textual's default Button is 3); got height={height}"
                )
E               AssertionError: @(80, 24): the entry chip must render as a single row (Textual's default Button is 3); got height=0
E               assert 0 == 1

    def test_at_i11_2_faulty_demo_errors_first(tmp_path: Path, demo_dir: Path) -> None:
        """The faulty demo lists its 8 errors on page 1, before the warnings; no later page
        shows an ERRORS group (base: 5 error rows on page 1, 3 on page 6)."""

        async def _go() -> None:
            app = S19TuiApp(base_dir=tmp_path)

                await _open_issues(pilot, app)

                )
                async for n, headers, groups in _pages(pilot, app, 8):
                    if n == 1:
                        assert headers == ["\u2717 ERRORS  (8)", "\u26a0 WARNINGS  (277)"], headers
                        assert [len(rows) for _, rows in groups] == [8, 32], [len(r) for _, r in groups]
                    else:
                        assert not any("ERRORS" in h for h in headers), (n, headers)

    def test_tc094_no_f841_finding_in_a2l() -> None:
        """``ruff --select F841`` reports zero findings on ``a2l.py``.

        Intent: R-A2L-010's numeric threshold is ``0 errors`` (pre-state: 1). This
        runs ruff over the whole module � so it catches BOTH the removed ``header``
        store and any accidental new dead store the edit might introduce � and
        asserts a clean exit. It **skips** (does not fail) when ruff is not
        importable, so a runtime-only environment (e.g. the CI ``tui-ci`` job, which
        installs no dev tooling) reports honest non-execution rather than a spurious
        failure � ruff is exercised in dev / pre-commit where it is installed.
        """
        import importlib.util

        import pytest

        if importlib.util.find_spec("ruff") is None:
            pytest.skip("ruff is not installed in this environment (runtime-only)")

        completed = subprocess.run(
            [sys.executable, "-m", "ruff", "check", "--select", "F841",
             str(_REPO_ROOT / "s19_app" / "tui" / "a2l.py")],
            cwd=_REPO_ROOT,
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
        )

    def test_at094_demo_parse_stable_after_dead_store_removal() -> None:
        """Parsing the demo A2L is unchanged by removing the dead ``header`` local.

        Intent: the deleted line sat between the ``header_meas``/``header_char``
        binding and their direct use in the tag build. This drives the shipped
        ``parse_a2l_file`` over the real demo fixture and pins invariants that flow
        through exactly that neighbourhood � the total tag count, MEASUREMENT header
        propagation (datatype + derived length), and CHARACTERISTIC parsing. A live
        line caught by the delete would perturb at least one of these.

        Batch-54 update: the old comment here claimed the demo "should parse only one
        CHARACTERISTIC" (the pre-batch-54 multi-line-header limitation). That is now
        FALSE � multi-line header assembly lands all 50 CHARACTERISTICs. The
        structural counts below (75 tags, 25 MEAS, 50 CHAR) are unchanged; the
        ``ASAM.C.VIRTUAL.ASCII`` char_type/length pins survive but are now a *genuine*
        parse (pre-batch-54 they were a ``ASCII /* � */`` comment-token artifact �
        char_type ASCII by luck, deposit garbage; now deposit resolves correctly).
        The 1\u219250 delta assertion lives in ``tests/test_a2l_multiline_headers.py``.
        These remain regression sentinels for the dead-store removal.
        """
        data = parse_a2l_file(_DEMO_A2L)
        tags = data["tags"]

        # Structural sentinel � the walk still emits every tag it did before.
        assert len(tags) == 75

        measurements = [t for t in tags if t.get("section") == "MEASUREMENT"]
        characteristics = [t for t in tags if t.get("section") == "CHARACTERISTIC"]
        assert len(measurements) == 25
        assert len(characteristics) == 50

        # header_meas propagation intact: MEASUREMENT length derivation still fires.
        meas_with_length = [t for t in measurements if t.get("length") is not None]
>       assert len(meas_with_length) == 24

    def test_r_crc_preview_002_5_8_zero_applied_restore_still_refreshes(
        tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
    ) -> None:
        """Verifies R-CRC-PREVIEW-002.5/8 (E3 A-2): after the apply of ``AA`` and one
        display (``0x08DAAFB7``), the entry moved to ``0x9000`` (outside the image)
        and Apply pressed, the status is ``Apply: 0 applied, 1 skipped, 0 blocked``,
        ``0x8003`` is back at ``03`` (the apply's journal restore), and the first
        display afterwards shows ``0x9C5BCBBD`` / ``0x2A8A3950``.

        Why: an apply that applies nothing has still undone the earlier apply in
        place. Base: the designer keeps ``0x08DAAFB7`` -> RED. Negative control: the
        hook gated on ``applied > 0`` -> RED. Drive note: the entry editor changes
        an entry by its address, so 'the address edited' is the Remove of the
        ``0x8003`` entry and the Add of ``0x9000`` = ``AA`` (same document)."""
        _set_quiet(monkeypatch, QUIET_SHORT_S)

        def make(folder: Path, image: Path):
            async def scenario(app, pilot, log):
                await _load_image_by_dialog(pilot, app, image)
                await _load_document(pilot, app, log, folder, "d.changes.json", [AA_ENTRY])
                assert await _apply(pilot, app, log, "Apply:") == "Apply: 1 applied, 0 skipped, 0 blocked"
                await _fresh_designer_display(pilot, app, AFTER_AA)
                await _entry_op(
                    pilot, app, log, "#patch_entry_remove_button", "0x8003", "", "Patch Editor: entry removed."
                )
                await _entry_op(
                    pilot, app, log, "#patch_entry_add_button", "0x9000", "AA", "Patch Editor: entry added."
                )
                before = designer_recomputes()  # E5 C-3: before the trigger
                status = await _apply(pilot, app, log, "Apply:")
                assert status == "Apply: 0 applied, 1 skipped, 0 blocked", status
                assert app.current_file.mem_map[0x8003] == 0x03, "the journal restore did not run"
                await _quiet(pilot)
                assert designer_recomputes() == before, "a hidden CRC Designer recomputed"
                await _press_rail(pilot, app, "0")
                await _expect_refresh(pilot, app, before, PRE, "zero-applied apply then display")

            return scenario

>       _reps(tmp_path, make)

    async def wait_until(
        pilot,
        predicate: Callable[[], bool],
        failure: Union[str, Callable[[], str]],
        *,
        timeout: float = WAIT_TIMEOUT_S,
        pause_first: bool = False,
    ) -> None:
        """Poll ``predicate`` until it is truthy, yielding to the app between
        checks; raise ``AssertionError`` with ``failure`` at the deadline.

        A callable ``failure`` is called only when this raises, so its text can
        show state accumulated up to the timeout.
        """
        deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout
        if pause_first:
            await pilot.pause()
        while True:
            if predicate():
                return
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
>               raise AssertionError(failure() if callable(failure) else failure)

    def test_control_other_patch_actions_do_not_mark_the_coverage_stale(

tmp_path = WindowsPath('[USER_HOME]/Github/s19_app-b103-g3-repro-residual/head/main/tmp/test_tc_b100_54_a2l_attach_ref0')
monkeypatch = <_pytest.monkeypatch.MonkeyPatch object at 0x0000018598D163F0>
gone = 'a2l_size'

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("gone", ["project_folder", "a2l_size"])
    def test_tc_b100_54_a2l_attach_refuses_when_its_folder_or_file_vanished(
        tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, gone: str
    ) -> None:
        """CR12b-2 / SE5-12b-2 (ruling E5, split by subject): before any busy
        run begins, ``load_a2l_from_path`` reads the open project's folder
        (``validate_project_files``) and the A2L's size (``stat``).
        - ``project_folder``: the open project ``gone`` was removed outside the
          app -> 1 line ``A2L NOT loaded: project 'gone' could not be read �
          check that the folder can be opened, then load again.``;
        - ``a2l_size``: the size ``stat`` raises ``OSError`` -> 1 line ``A2L
          file not found: <typed>``.
        Both: nothing raised out of the request, 0 begins, no run left, the app
        running.

        Why: from the Load dialog or a deferred callback the raise ends the app
        and the unsaved session is lost; a size read as 0 would skip the
        over-cap rule. Negative control (81ac4c0): both calls are unguarded ->
        ``FileNotFoundError`` / ``OSError`` leaves the request -> RED."""
        base = tmp_path / "app"
        a2l = tmp_path / "src" / "firmware.a2l"
        a2l.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
        shutil.copyfile(CASE01 / "firmware.a2l", a2l)
        begins = Spy(monkeypatch, S19TuiApp, "_busy_begin_action")
        if gone == "a2l_size":
            real_stat = pathlib.Path.stat

            def _stat(self, *args, **kwargs):
                if sys._getframe(1).f_code.co_name == "load_a2l_from_path":
                    raise OSError(5, "synthetic size read failure")
                return real_stat(self, *args, **kwargs)

            monkeypatch.setattr(pathlib.Path, "stat", _stat)

        async def scenario(app, pilot, log):
            if gone == "project_folder":
                app.current_project = "gone"
                app.current_project_dir = app.workarea / "gone"
            mark = len(log.messages)
            try:
                app.load_a2l_from_path(a2l)
                raised = None
            except BaseException as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 � the RED shape
                raised = type(exc).__name__
            for _ in range(3):
                await pilot.pause()
            return raised, log.since(mark), list(app._busy.runs), running(app)

        raised, lines, runs, alive = run(base, scenario)
        expected = (
            "A2L NOT loaded: project 'gone' could not be read � check that the folder "
            "can be opened, then load again."
            if gone == "project_folder"
            else f"A2L file not found: {a2l}"
        )
        assert raised is None, f"{raised} left load_a2l_from_path"
>       assert lines == [expected], lines
E       AssertionError: ['A2L loaded: firmware.a2l']
E       assert ['A2L loaded: firmware.a2l'] == ['A2L file no...firmware.a2l']

    def test_r_crc_preview_002_5_9_save_back_raising_after_the_adopt_ends_the_app(
        tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
    ) -> None:
        """Case R-CRC-PREVIEW-002.5/9 (E5 C-6, robustness; partial: the fault is
        injected): after the apply of ``AA``, a display and the entry edited to
        ``BB``, a save-back whose emit raises after ``save_patched`` adopted the
        re-derived bytes -> ``0x8003`` holds ``BB``, and the app ends with that
        error (return code 1, the error raised out of the run), so the CRC
        Designer can never show the pre-write CRC (E5-S2 ruling: no hook needed).

        Standing guard (passes on the base). Negative control: the save-back
        handler swallowing the error -> the app keeps running -> RED (M30)."""
        _set_quiet(monkeypatch, QUIET_SHORT_S)

        def raise_after_adopt(self, *args, **kwargs):
            raise RuntimeError("probe after adopt")

        monkeypatch.setattr(change_service_module.ChangeService, "emit_and_verify", raise_after_adopt)
        seen = {}

        def make(folder: Path, image: Path):
            async def scenario(app, pilot, log):

                await _load_image_by_dialog(pilot, app, image)

                await _fresh_designer_display(pilot, app, AFTER_AA)
                await _entry_op(
                    pilot, app, log, "#patch_entry_edit_button", "0x8003", "BB", "Patch Editor: entry updated."
                )
                await _confirm_save_back(pilot, app, "fixture-raise.s19", by_keys=False)
                for _ in range(200):
                    if app._exception is not None:
                        break
                    await asyncio.sleep(0.05)
                seen["byte"] = app.current_file.mem_map[0x8003]
                assert app._exception is not None, "the save-back error was swallowed; the app runs on"

            return scenario

    async def wait_until(
        pilot,
        predicate: Callable[[], bool],
        failure: Union[str, Callable[[], str]],
        *,
        timeout: float = WAIT_TIMEOUT_S,
        pause_first: bool = False,
    ) -> None:
        """Poll ``predicate`` until it is truthy, yielding to the app between
        checks; raise ``AssertionError`` with ``failure`` at the deadline.

        A callable ``failure`` is called only when this raises, so its text can
        show state accumulated up to the timeout.
        """
        deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout
        if pause_first:
            await pilot.pause()

            if predicate():
                return
